{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"ironies","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["The quality of a statement that, when taken in context, may actually mean something different from, or the opposite of, what is written literally; the use of words expressing something other than their literal intention, often in a humorous context."],"tags":["countable","rhetoric","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The quality of a statement that, when taken in context, may actually mean something different from, or the opposite of, what is written literally; the use of words expressing something other than their literal intention, often in a humorous context.","An ironic statement."],"tags":["countable","rhetoric"]},{"glosses":["Dramatic irony: a theatrical effect in which the meaning of a situation, or some incongruity in the plot, is understood by the audience, but not by the characters in the play."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Socratic irony: ignorance feigned for the purpose of confounding or provoking an antagonist."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Contradiction between circumstances and expectations; condition contrary to what might be expected."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈaɪə.ɹə.ni/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈaɪ.ɹə.ni/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈaɪ.ɚ.ni/","tags":["Canada","US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɑɪ.ɹən.i/","tags":["Australia"]}],"upstream_line":251431,"upstream_offset":1247415635,"upstream_record_sha256":"fecfd169ff696c507532465cf2a8a651a0877dc40d0cff74866d1c5c652933be","word":"irony"}

{"antonyms":[{"word":"non-rhotic"}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Rhotic speech is common in Ireland, Scotland, much of the United States, Canada, West Country England, and many parts of the north and west of England.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["That allows the phoneme /ɹ/ to follow a vowel, as in bar (/bɑːɹ/) and bard or barred (/bɑːɹd/); (of an English speaker) who speaks with such an accent."],"raw_tags":["of an English accent"],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"In the IPA, a rhotic vowel (aka R-coloured vowel, retroflex vowel, vocalic r or rhotacised vowel) is indicated by the affixing of a hook diacritic ( ˞ ) to the right of the regular symbol for the vowel. The rhotic consonants are /r/, /ɾ/, /ɹ/, /ɻ/, /ʀ/, /ʁ/, /ɽ/ and /ɺ/.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Having a sound quality associated with the letter R; having the sound of any of certain IPA symbols, including /ɹ/, /ɻ/, /ɚ/, /ɝ/ and /r/."],"raw_tags":["of a phoneme"],"synonyms":[{"word":"retroflex"}],"tags":["not-comparable"],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","phonetics","phonology","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"rōʹtĭk"},{"ipa":"/ˈɹəʊ.tɪk/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[ˈɹəʊ̯.t(ʰ)ɪk]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈɹoʊ.tɪk/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈɹoʊ̯.ɾɪk]","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":169198,"upstream_offset":797090580,"upstream_record_sha256":"63acbf34dbe153b59610b96228791efa64fb8e9944a8386be14fa1617b2523b9","word":"rhotic"}
